Here's a summary of the bug and fix:\n\n## Root cause\n\nThe issue is a regression introduced in requests 2.27.0 (PR #5917, \"Improved proxy parsing for proxy URLs missing a scheme\"), which rewrote `prepend_scheme_if_needed()` in `requests/utils.py` to use urllib3's `parse_url` instead of stdlib `urlparse`. That rewrite was meant to address the `urlparse` changes the reporter linked to (Python 3.8.12+).\n\nurllib3's `parse_url` returns the credentials as a separate `auth` component, and its `netloc` does **not** include the `user:pass@` prefix:\n\n```\nparse_url('http://user:pass@proxy:8080') -> scheme=http, auth='user:pass', netloc='proxy:8080'\n```\n\nWhen `prepend_scheme_if_needed` rebuilt the URL via `urlunparse((scheme, netloc, ...))`, the `auth` portion was silently dropped. Proxy URLs with embedded credentials (e.g. `http://user:pass@proxy:8080` passed via the `proxies` dict) then lost their credentials before `get_auth_from_url()` could extract them, so no `Proxy-Authorization` header was ever sent → the proxy returned **407 Proxy Authentication Required**.\n\nI verified this directly:\n\n```\nprepend_scheme_if_needed('http://user:pass@example.com:8080', 'http')\n# before fix: 'http://example.com:8080'   (auth dropped)\n# after fix:  'http://user:pass@example.com:8080'\n```\n\n## Fix\n\nIn `requests/utils.py` (`prepend_scheme_if_needed`), re-attach the `auth` component to the netloc when present — matching upstream PR #6028:\n\n```python\nif auth:\n    # parse_url doesn't provide the netloc with auth\n    # so we'll add it ourselves.\n    netloc = '@'.join([auth, netloc])\n```\n\nAlso added regression test cases to `tests/test_utils.py::test_prepend_scheme_if_needed` for URLs with `user:pass@` and `user@` credentials.\n\n## Verification\n\n- New/updated `test_prepend_scheme_if_needed` parametrized tests: **5 passed**\n- All proxy-related tests in `tests/test_requests.py`: **15 passed**\n- Full suite: 548 passed, 12 skipped, 1 xfailed; the single failure (`test_redirecting_to_bad_url` with `localhost:-1`) and one error (`test_https_warnings`) are pre-existing environment issues (Python 3.11 stdlib port-parsing behavior / missing module) — confirmed they fail identically on the unmodified code."}],"api":"openai-completions","provider":"opencode-go","model":"deepseek-v4-flash","usage":{"input":455,"output":925,"cacheRead":22912,"cacheWrite":0,"reasoning":321,"totalTokens":24292,"cost":{"input":0.00003185,"output":0.0001295,"cacheRead":0.0000320768,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0.0001934268}},"stopReason":"stop","timestamp":1786864261497,"responseId":"72ad8ce8-7479-43fb-bcc2-7707f6b1bde3","rawStopReason":"stop"}}

Let me summarize.\n\nRoot cause:\n- Requests 2.27.0 (PR #5917) rewrote `prepend_scheme_if_needed` in `requests/utils.py` to use urllib3's `parse_url` instead of stdlib `urlparse` (to address Python 3.9+ `urlparse` changes — this is what the reporter linked as the \"Python 3.8.12\" change).\n- urllib3's `parse_url` returns the credentials as a separate `auth` component and its `netloc` does NOT include the `user:pass@` prefix.\n- When `prepend_scheme_if_needed` reconstructs the URL with `urlunparse((scheme, netloc, path, '', query, fragment))`, the auth portion was dropped.\n- Proxy URLs with embedded credentials (e.g. `http://user:pass@proxy:port`) therefore lost their credentials before `get_auth_from_url` could extract them, so no `Proxy-Authorization` header was sent → HTTP 407.\n\nFix:\n- Re-add `auth` to `netloc` when present before reconstructing the URL.\n\nTests:\n- Added parametrized cases to `test_prepend_scheme_if_needed` covering URLs with user:pass and user-only auth.\n\nAll proxy-related tests pass (15 passed), full suite: 548 passed, 1 pre-existing failure + 1 env error unrelated to the change.","thinkingSignature":"reasoning_content"},{"type":"text","text":"Done. Here's a summary of the bug and fix:\n\n## Root cause\n\nThe issue is a regression introduced in requests 2.27.0 (PR #5917, \"Improved proxy parsing for proxy URLs missing a scheme\"), which rewrote `prepend_scheme_if_needed()` in `requests/utils.py` to use urllib3's `parse_url` instead of stdlib `urlparse`. That rewrite was meant to address the `urlparse` changes the reporter linked to (Python 3.8.12+).\n\nurllib3's `parse_url` returns the credentials as a separate `auth` component, and its `netloc` does **not** include the `user:pass@` prefix:\n\n```\nparse_url('http://user:pass@proxy:8080') -> scheme=http, auth='user:pass', netloc='proxy:8080'\n```\n\nWhen `prepend_scheme_if_needed` rebuilt the URL via `urlunparse((scheme, netloc, ...))`, the `auth` portion was silently dropped. Proxy URLs with embedded credentials (e.g. `http://user:pass@proxy:8080` passed via the `proxies` dict) then lost their credentials before `get_auth_from_url()` could extract them, so no `Proxy-Authorization` header was ever sent → the proxy returned **407 Proxy Authentication Required**.\n\nI verified this directly:\n\n```\nprepend_scheme_if_needed('http://user:pass@example.com:8080', 'http')\n# before fix: 'http://example.com:8080'   (auth dropped)\n# after fix:  'http://user:pass@example.com:8080'\n```\n\n## Fix\n\nIn `requests/utils.py` (`prepend_scheme_if_needed`), re-attach the `auth` component to the netloc when present — matching upstream PR #6028:\n\n```python\nif auth:\n    # parse_url doesn't provide the netloc with auth\n    # so we'll add it ourselves.\n    netloc = '@'.join([auth, netloc])\n```\n\nAlso added regression test cases to `tests/test_utils.py::test_prepend_scheme_if_needed` for URLs with `user:pass@` and `user@` credentials.\n\n## Verification\n\n- New/updated `test_prepend_scheme_if_needed` parametrized tests: **5 passed**\n- All proxy-related tests in `tests/test_requests.py`: **15 passed**\n- Full suite: 548 passed, 12 skipped, 1 xfailed; the single failure (`test_redirecting_to_bad_url` with `localhost:-1`) and one error (`test_https_warnings`) are pre-existing environment issues (Python 3.11 stdlib port-parsing behavior / missing module) — confirmed they fail identically on the unmodified code."}],"api":"openai-completions","provider":"opencode-go","model":"deepseek-v4-flash","usage":{"input":455,"output":925,"cacheRead":22912,"cacheWrite":0,"reasoning":321,"totalTokens":24292,"cost":{"input":0.00003185,"output":0.0001295,"cacheRead":0.0000320768,"cacheWrite":0,"total":0.0001934268}},"stopReason":"stop","timestamp":1786864261497,"responseId":"72ad8ce8-7479-43fb-bcc2-7707f6b1bde3","rawStopReason":"stop"}],"willRetry":false}
